   4: This register tracks system-wide enforcement tests addressing non-DreamState gaps (e.g., authentication, workflow, latency, accessibility, onboarding) to ensure Codex v6.1.4 compliance and emotional auditability across CanAI. It complements `/docs/dreamstate-test-mappings.md` by covering tests outside the DreamState suite, replacing verbose entries in `/cursor/auto-actions.log.md`. The register defends critical system paths, asserts key functions, and aligns with Codex pillars: Security, Operational Resilience, Emotional UX Fidelity, Accessibility, and Agent Enablement. Cursor must index this file to validate system-wide coverage and execute directives with confidence.
   5  

  27  - **Scope:** Tests defend critical system paths outside `/tests/dreamstate/`, focusing on real system logic and emotional outcomes (Calm Trust, Clarity & Trust, Strategic Continuity, Inclusion).
  28: - **Real System Bound:** All `COMPLETE` tests use real system imports, validated in `/cursor/mock-integrity-audit.md` [2025-05-21T15:00Z]. `TODO` and `UNTAPPED` tests are pending full implementation.
  29  - **CI Integration:** Tests run in `.github/workflows/codex-enforcement.yml`, failing on pillar violations. `TODO` and `UNTAPPED` tests will be integrated upon completion.

  120  - **What caused the apparent log overwrite behavior?**
  121:   - Prior audit log entries in `/cursor/auto-actions.log.md` were appended as standalone JSON code blocks. While technically correct for append operations, many Markdown renderers and agent viewers (including Cursor, VS Code, and GitHub) visually collapse or only display the last JSON block, creating the false impression that previous logs were overwritten.
  122  

  140  - **What patterns were noticed?**
  141:   - .cursor folder contains all required Codex audit intent files (README.md, intent-token.json, log-expectation.md), system identity anchors (self-awareness.json, codex-traits.json), and references to boot sequence routines.
  142    - Boot logic is modular, versioned, and references meta-agent scaffolds and fallback handlers.

  150  - **Were any files auto-generated by Sentinel?**
  151:   - Yes. README.md, intent-token.json, and log-expectation.md were auto-generated using canonical templates.

docs\reference\dreamstate-test-mapping.md:
   3  ## Overview
   4: This document serves as the canonical reference for mapping the 33 DreamState v1.0.4 tests to legacy test files, system paths, and Codex v6.1.4 pillars, ensuring auditability, continuity, and emotional fidelity for CanAI. It replaces verbose entries in `/cursor/auto-actions.log.md`, enabling Cursor to efficiently validate test coverage and execute directives with confidence. The mappings defend critical system paths, assert key functions, and align with Codex pillars: Emotional UX Fidelity, Trust, Operational Resilience, Fallback Resilience, and Inclusion. Cursor must index this file for all test-related queries to ensure precise implementation and auditability.
   5  
   6  ## Test Mapping Table
   7: The table below details each DreamState test, its legacy mappings, actions taken, rationale, confidence level, defended system paths, asserted functions, and Codex pillar alignment. Confidence levels reflect mapping accuracy and coverage completeness, with escalation to Cofounder required for <95% confidence. All tests are Real System Bound, per `/cursor/mock-integrity-audit.md`.
   8  

  54  - **Escalation:** Escalate to Cofounder for review if a test’s confidence is <95%, mapping ambiguity arises, or pillar violations are detected.
  55: - **Logging:** Log all test mapping changes in `/cursor/auto-actions.log.md`, referencing this file for traceability.
  56: - **Updates:** Propose new test mappings or updates via `/cursor/auto-actions.log.md` with Cofounder approval.
  57  - **CI Integration:** Tests are validated in `.github/workflows/codex-enforcement.yml`, failing on pillar violations or missing coverage.

  30  - CI must fail on any pillar violation, with escalation to Cofounder.

  34  - **Escalation:** Pause and escalate to Cofounder for review if context is unclear, confidence is <95%, or pillar violations are detected.
  35: - **Logging:** Log all actions in `/cursor/auto-actions.log.md`, referencing this directive for traceability.
  36: - **Updates:** Propose directive updates via `/cursor/auto-actions.log.md` with Cofounder approval.
  37
